   <h4 class='subsectionHead' id='implementation-details'><span class='titlemark'>A.2    </span> <a id='x1-33000A.2'></a>Implementation details</h4>
<!-- l. 435 --><p class='noindent'>When choosing models for NLI and semantic similarity, we considered three criteria: (i) whether a public
pre-trained checkpoint was available; (ii) model size, since all the required models had to fit on our GPU;
and (iii) performance, measured through correlation with human evaluation.
</p><!-- l. 437 --><p class='indent'>   For semantic similarity, we used <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>GIST-Embedding-v0</span> [<a href='#Xsolatorio2024gistembed'>12</a>]. For NLI, we used <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>ModernCE-base</span> [<a href='#Xmoderncenli2025'>9</a>].
During evaluation, we used <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>stella_en_1.5B_v5</span> [<a href='#Xzhang2025jasperstelladistillationsota'>20</a>] to measure semantic similarity, since we no longer
needed to keep several models loaded on the same GPU.
</p>
   <h5 class='subsubsectionHead' id='natural-language-inference-nli-gate'><span class='titlemark'>A.2.1    </span> <a id='x1-34000A.2.1'></a>Natural Language Inference (NLI) Gate</h5>
<!-- l. 442 --><p class='noindent'>Embedding similarity alone can assign high scores to rewrites that preserve the broad topic of a query while changing
important logical constraints. To reduce this failure mode, we add an NLI gate that checks whether the clean query
<!-- l. 442 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msub><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub></math></span> and generated
paraphrase <!-- l. 442 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msubsup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>adv</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup></math></span>
contradict each other. The NLI model returns contradiction probabilities in both directions:
</p><table class='equation-star'><tr><td>
<!-- l. 443 --><math class='equation' display='block' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow>
       <msubsup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>fwd</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'>
<mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>contradiction</mi></mstyle><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∣</mo><msub><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>adv</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mspace class='qquad' width='2em'></mspace><msubsup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow>
<mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>bwd</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'>
<mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>contradiction</mi></mstyle><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∣</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>adv</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow>
<mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>.</mo>
</mrow></math></td></tr></table>
<!-- l. 447 --><p class='indent'>   The forward pass catches paraphrases that introduce unsupported assumptions, while the backward
pass catches paraphrases that drop constraints from the original query. We then define a conservative
bidirectional non-contradiction score: </p><table class='equation-star'><tr><td>
<!-- l. 448 --><math class='equation' display='block' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow>
                          <msubsup><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup> <mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>:</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo><mi class='qopname'> min</mi><mo> ⁡<!-- FUNCTION APPLICATION --> </mo><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <munderover accent='false' accentunder='false'><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow>
<mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>fwd</mi></mstyle></mrow></munderover><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <msubsup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow>
<mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>bwd</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>.</mo>
</mrow></math></td></tr></table>
<!-- l. 451 --><p class='indent'>   Taking the minimum makes the gate reject a pair if either direction appears contradictory, which is
appropriate because paraphrase equivalence is stricter than one-way entailment.
</p><!-- l. 453 --><p class='indent'>   For the multiplicative reward, this score is converted into a smooth gate: </p><table class='equation-star'><tr><td>
<!-- l. 454 --><math class='equation' display='block' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow>
                             <msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>:</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mi>σ</mi><mspace class='negthinspace' width='-0.17em'></mspace> <mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>(</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub></mrow>
      <mrow><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac>       </mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'>)</mo></mrow> <mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>.</mo>
</mrow></math></td></tr></table>
<!-- l. 457 --><p class='indent'>   When <!-- l. 457 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msubsup><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup></math></span> is above
the NLI floor <!-- l. 457 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msub><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub></math></span>, the
gate remains close to <!-- l. 457 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mn>1</mn></math></span>
and the routing reward is mostly preserved. When it is below the floor, the gate moves toward
<!-- l. 457 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mn>0</mn></math></span>,
reducing positive rewards for likely semantic drift. In the full reward this effect is sign-aware: low NLI
confidence suppresses positive core rewards, but amplifies negative ones. Thus, the paraphraser
is discouraged from exploiting router weaknesses by changing the user’s intent, while still
receiving credit for benign lexical or syntactic rewrites that are not perfectly captured by cosine
similarity.
</p>
   <h5 class='subsubsectionHead' id='multiplicative-reward-formulation'><span class='titlemark'>A.2.2    </span> <a id='x1-35000A.2.2'></a>Multiplicative Reward Formulation</h5>
<!-- l. 462 --><p class='noindent'>The core reward <!-- l. 462 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>core</mi></mstyle></mrow></msup></math></span> relies on soft
similarity floors and sigmoid gates <!-- l. 462 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mi>σ</mi></math></span>:
</p><table class='equation-star'><tr><td>
<!-- l. 463 --><math class='equation' display='block' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow>
                <msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>core</mi></mstyle></mrow></msup> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow>
<mi>x</mi></mrow></msub> <mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>[</mo><mrow><mi>z</mi> <mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>(</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow></msub><mi>x</mi> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>+</mo> <mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <mi>x</mi><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo></mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'>)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'>]</mo></mrow> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>+</mo> <mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo><mover accent='true'><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo accent='true'>~</mo></mover><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo><mi>x</mi>
</mrow></math></td></tr></table>
<!-- l. 466 --><p class='indent'>   where <!-- l. 466 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mi>x</mi></math></span> is the routing reward
excluding the flip bonus, <!-- l. 466 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mi>z</mi> <mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>:</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mover accent='true'><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo accent='true'>~</mo></mover> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>sim</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub></mrow></math></span> is the
margin above similarity floor <!-- l. 466 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msub><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>sim</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub></math></span>,
and the gating functions are <!-- l. 466 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>:</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mi>σ</mi><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mi>z</mi><mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>∕</mo><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo></mrow></math></span>
and <!-- l. 466 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>:</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mi>σ</mi><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>∕</mo><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo></mrow></math></span>.
</p><!-- l. 468 --><p class='indent'>   The NLI term <!-- l. 468 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msup><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msup></math></span>
adjusts the core reward based on contradiction: </p><table class='equation-star'><tr><td>
<!-- l. 469 --><math class='equation' display='block' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow>
                         <msup><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msup> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow>
<mi>r</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>+</mo> <mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mn>2</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo>
</mrow></math></td></tr></table>
<!-- l. 472 --><p class='indent'>   where <!-- l. 472 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mi>σ</mi><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>core</mi></mstyle></mrow></msup><mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>∕</mo><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo></mrow></math></span>
switches behavior depending on the core reward’s sign and
<!-- l. 472 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msub><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>NLI</mi></mstyle><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub></math></span> is the
NLI gate. The sigmoid gates provide smooth transitions between these regimes, preserving reward
continuity and avoiding the noisy signals that would arise from hard thresholding.
</p>
   <h5 class='subsubsectionHead' id='attack-success-rate-formulation'><span class='titlemark'>A.2.3    </span> <a id='x1-36000A.2.3'></a>Attack Success Rate Formulation</h5>
<!-- l. 477 --><p class='noindent'>The Attack Success Rate (<!-- l. 477 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msub><mrow><mstyle class='text'><mtext>ASR</mtext></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></msub></math></span>)
for a given router <!-- l. 477 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mi>k</mi></math></span>
measures the fraction of queries originally assigned to the weak model that
are successfully redirected to the strong model via the adversarial paraphrase
<!-- l. 477 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>adv</mi></mstyle></mrow></msup></math></span>. It is
defined as: </p><table class='equation-star'><tr><td>
<!-- l. 478 --><math class='equation' display='block' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><msub><mrow>
             <mstyle class='text'><mtext>ASR</mtext></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mfrac><mrow><munderover accent='false' accentunder='false'><mrow><mo>∑</mo>
  </mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></munderover><mi>𝕀</mi><mstyle mathsize='1.61em'><mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>[</mo><mrow></mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'></mo></mrow></mstyle><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∈</mo><msubsup><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='script'><mi>M</mi></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>weak</mi></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mi>k</mi><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><mspace class='nbsp' width='0.33em'></mspace> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>∧</mo><mspace class='nbsp' width='0.33em'></mspace><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>adv</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∈</mo><msubsup><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='script'><mi>M</mi></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>strong</mi></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mi>k</mi><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><mstyle mathsize='1.61em'><mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>]</mo><mrow></mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'></mo></mrow></mstyle></mrow>
                     <mrow><munderover accent='false' accentunder='false'><mrow><mo> ∑</mo>
  </mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></munderover><mi>𝕀</mi><mstyle mathsize='1.61em'><mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>[</mo><mrow></mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'></mo></mrow></mstyle><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∈</mo><msubsup><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='script'><mi>M</mi></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>weak</mi></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><mi>k</mi><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><mstyle mathsize='1.61em'><mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>]</mo><mrow></mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'></mo></mrow></mstyle></mrow></mfrac>
</mrow></math></td></tr></table>
   <h4 class='subsectionHead' id='grpo'><span class='titlemark'>A.3    </span> <a id='x1-37000A.3'></a>GRPO</h4>
<!-- l. 486 --><p class='noindent'>We define: </p><table class='equation-star'><tr><td>
<!-- l. 487 --><math class='equation' display='block' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'>
 <mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>{</mo><mrow> <mtable align='axis' class='array' columnlines='none' displaystyle='true' equalcolumns='false' equalrows='false' rowlines='none' style=''> <mtr class='array-row'><mtd class='array-td' columnalign='left'><msubsup><mrow><mi>ℓ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗𝑡</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>PPO</mi></mstyle></mrow></msubsup> <mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>:</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo><mi class='qopname'>min</mi><mo> ⁡<!-- FUNCTION APPLICATION --> </mo> <mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>(</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ρ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗𝑡</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>clip</mi></mstyle><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>ρ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗𝑡</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <mi>𝜖</mi><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mn>1</mn> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>+</mo> <mi>𝜖</mi><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'>)</mo></mrow>           <mspace class='quad' width='1em'></mspace></mtd>
</mtr>  <mtr class='array-row'><mtd class='array-td' columnalign='left'><msub><mrow><mi>ρ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗𝑡</mi></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>:</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mi class='loglike'>exp</mi><mo>⁡<!-- FUNCTION APPLICATION --></mo><!-- nolimits --><mspace class='negthinspace' width='-0.17em'></mspace> <mrow><mo fence='true' form='prefix'>(</mo><mrow><mstyle><mi class='loglike'>log</mi><mo>⁡<!-- FUNCTION APPLICATION --></mo></mstyle><!-- nolimits --><msub><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>𝜃</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗𝑡</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∣</mo><msub><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗</mi><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>&lt;</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo><mstyle><mi class='loglike'>log</mi><mo>⁡<!-- FUNCTION APPLICATION --></mo></mstyle><!-- nolimits --><msub><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle mathvariant='normal'><mi>old</mi></mstyle></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-open' stretchy='false'>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗𝑡</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∣</mo><msub><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant='italic'>𝑖𝑗</mi><mo class='MathClass-punc' stretchy='false'>,</mo><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>&lt;</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></msub><mo class='MathClass-close' stretchy='false'>)</mo></mrow><mo fence='true' form='postfix'>)</mo></mrow><mspace class='quad' class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>end</span> <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>for</span>         </span><br class='algorithmic' /><span class='label-13.13995pt'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-90'>17:</span> </span> <span class='algorithmic'> <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>end</span> <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>for</span></span>
</table></span></span></div>
   </figure>
   </div>
   <h4 class='subsectionHead' id='ablations1'><span class='titlemark'>A.4    </span> <a id='x1-38000A.4'></a>Ablations</h4>
   <h5 class='subsubsectionHead' id='router-pool1'><span class='titlemark'>A.4.1    </span> <a id='x1-39000A.4.1'></a>Router Pool</h5>
<!-- l. 590 --><p class='noindent'>All selected routers are open-source and pretrained, which supports reproducibility and ensures easy
access for future evaluation and comparison.
</p><!-- l. 592 --><p class='indent'>   </p><figure class='float' id='x1-39001r2'><span id='router-pool-used-throughout-the-experiments-training-routers-provide-reward-signals-during-rl-while-transfer-routers-are-held-out-until-evaluation'></span>
<div class='tabular'>
 <table class='tabular' id='TBL-5'><colgroup id='TBL-5-1g'><col id='TBL-5-1' /><col id='TBL-5-2' /><col id='TBL-5-3' /><col id='TBL-5-4' /></colgroup><tr id='TBL-5-1-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-5-1-1' style='text-align:left;'><!-- l. 599 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Pool</span>            </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-1-2' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 599 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Router</span>                       </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-1-3' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 599 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Source</span>                                  </p></td><td class='td10' id='TBL-5-1-4' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 599 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Role in the experiment</span>                                                                       </p></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-5-2-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-5-2-1' style='text-align:left;'><!-- l. 601 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Training</span>       </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-2-2' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 601 --><p class='noindent'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>routellm_bert</span>    </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-2-3' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 601 --><p class='noindent'><a class='url' href='https://huggingface.co/routellm/bert_gpt4_augmented'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>https://huggingface.co/routellm/bert_gpt4_augmented</span></a> </p> </td><td class='td10' id='TBL-5-2-4' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 601 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Fast local classifier based on BERT and trained on preference-style
  routing data. Because it is cheap enough to batch heavily during RL
  training, it also enables quick experiments.</span>                                       </p></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-5-3-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-5-3-1' style='text-align:left;'><!-- l. 602 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Training</span>       </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-3-2' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 602 --><p class='noindent'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>routellm_causal</span>  </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-3-3' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 602 --><p class='noindent'><a class='url' href='https://huggingface.co/routellm/causal_llm_gpt4_augmented'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>https://huggingface.co/routellm/causal_llm_gpt4_augmented</span></a> </p> </td><td class='td10' id='TBL-5-3-4' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 602 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Higher-capacity LLM-based RouteLLM classifier. It is much more
  memory-heavy, but gives the training pool a qualitatively different
  boundary from the BERT classifier.</span>                                                   </p></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-5-4-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-5-4-1' style='text-align:left;'><!-- l. 603 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Training</span>       </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-4-2' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 603 --><p class='noindent'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>chayan</span>           </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-4-3' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 603 --><p class='noindent'><a class='url' href='https://huggingface.co/adaptive-classifier/chayan'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>https://huggingface.co/adaptive-classifier/chayan</span></a> </p> </td><td class='td10' id='TBL-5-4-4' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 603 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Adaptive K-NN classifier of BERT-base-uncased embeddings with
  post-training  confidence  calibration.  It  is  included  in  training
  because  it’s  outside  the  RouteLLM  routers  family,  so  successful
  flips are less likely to be RouteLLM-specific.</span>                                   </p></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-5-5-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-5-5-1' style='text-align:left;'><!-- l. 604 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Transfer</span>       </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-5-2' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 604 --><p class='noindent'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>r2</span>               </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-5-3' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 604 --><p class='noindent'><a class='url' href='https://huggingface.co/JiaqiXue/R2-Router-RouterArena'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>https://huggingface.co/JiaqiXue/R2-Router-RouterArena</span></a> </p> </td><td class='td10' id='TBL-5-5-4' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 604 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Held out as the strongest transfer  target.  It  embeds  each  query,
  uses  small  MLP  predictors  at  different  token  budgets  to  estimate
  each  available  model’s  quality-cost  curve,  then  chooses  the  best
  model–budget pair. This makes it a clean test of whether the learned
  attack generalizes beyond the other router architectures.</span>                  </p></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-5-6-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-5-6-1' style='text-align:left;'><!-- l. 605 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Transfer</span>       </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-6-2' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 605 --><p class='noindent'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>routellm_mf</span>      </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-6-3' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 605 --><p class='noindent'><a class='url' href='https://huggingface.co/routellm/mf_gpt4_augmented'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>https://huggingface.co/routellm/mf_gpt4_augmented</span></a> </p> </td><td class='td10' id='TBL-5-6-4' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 605 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Matrix-factorization RouteLLM  router.  It  is  kept  out  of  training
  because  it  depends  on  OpenAI  embeddings,  which  would  have
  made training expensive.</span>                                                                    </p></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-5-7-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-5-7-1' style='text-align:left;'><!-- l. 606 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Transfer</span>       </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-7-2' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 606 --><p class='noindent'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>routellm_sw</span>      </p></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-5-7-3' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 606 --><p class='noindent'><a class='url' href='https://github.com/lm-sys/RouteLLM'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-80'>https://github.com/lm-sys/RouteLLM</span></a> </p> </td><td class='td10' id='TBL-5-7-4' style='text-align:left;'> <!-- l. 606 --><p class='noindent'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Weighted Elo / similarity-weighted RouteLLM router; kept out of
  training because it depends on OpenAI embeddings, which would
  have made training expensive.</span>                                                           </p></td>
</tr></table>                                                                              </div>
<figcaption class='caption'><span class='id'>Table 2: </span><span class='content'>Router pool used throughout the experiments. Training routers provide reward signals
during RL, while transfer routers are held out until evaluation.
</span></figcaption><!-- tex4ht:label?: x1339001r2  -->
   </figure>
   <h5 class='subsubsectionHead' id='reproducibility'><span class='titlemark'>A.4.2    </span> <a id='x1-40000A.4.2'></a>Reproducibility</h5>
<!-- l. 616 --><p class='noindent'>To ensure reproducibility, this section provides the exact configurations used in our training runs. We
have also publicly released our training data, trained paraphraser checkpoints, and ablation
runs at <a class='url' href='https://huggingface.co/datasets/MauroPello/llm-routing-attack-data'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>https://huggingface.co/datasets/MauroPello/llm-routing-attack-data</span></a> and
<a class='url' href='https://huggingface.co/MauroPello/llm-routing-attack-paraphrasers'><span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>https://huggingface.co/MauroPello/llm-routing-attack-paraphrasers</span></a>.
</p>
   <div class='table'>
<!-- l. 618 --><p class='indent'>   </p><figure class='float' id='x1-40001r3'><span id='configuration-abbreviations-used-in-tablesref-tabablationconfigs-andref-tabsinglerouterconfigs-rows-marked-with-indicate-runs-that-are-considered-as-our-final-models'></span>
<div class='tabular'>
 <table class='tabular' id='TBL-6'><colgroup id='TBL-6-1g'><col id='TBL-6-1' /><col id='TBL-6-2' /><col id='TBL-6-3' /></colgroup><tr id='TBL-6-1-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-1-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Abbreviation   </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-1-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>              <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Run Name                             </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-1-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                                                  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Description                                                                                                                                                                   </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-2-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-2-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>    <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>BASE         </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-2-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>BASE                                </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-2-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                                 <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Base pretrained T5-chatGPT-paraphraser model [</span><a href='#Xchatgpt_paraphraser'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>16</span></a><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>].                                                                                                                                  </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-3-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-3-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>FT*          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-3-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>            <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>FINETUNED</span><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>*                        </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-3-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Baseline configuration. Optimal hyperparameters selected through experimentation.                                                                                                          </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-4-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-4-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>    <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>AGG*         </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-4-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>            <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>AGGRESSIVE</span><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>*                       </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-4-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                        <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests limits of policy deviation with high LR/PPO clip and low KL penalty.                                                                                                                </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-5-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-5-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>LSF           </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-5-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>LOW_SIM_FLOOR_ABLATION          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-5-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests if a lower similarity floor (</span><!-- l. 629 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle class='text'><mtext>sim</mtext></mstyle></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mn>0.80</mn></mrow></math></span><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>) improves reward without semantic collapse.   </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-6-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-6-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>NFB          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-6-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>NO_FLIP_BONUS_ABLATION          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-6-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                         <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests if confidence deltas alone suffice without explicit class-flip bonuses.                                                                                                                  </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-7-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-7-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>NNLI         </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-7-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>         <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>NO_NLI_ABLATION                   </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-7-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                       <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests if NLI entailment checks are actually necessary to prevent hallucinations.                                                                                                             </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-8-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-8-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>SRL          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-8-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>    <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>SIMULTANEOUS_RL_ABLATION       </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-8-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Considers all routers simultaneously in the reward, instead of the sequential setting.                                                                                                          </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-9-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-9-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>    <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>ZWSA        </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-9-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>ZERO_W_SIM_ADDITIVE_ABLATION   </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-9-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                                <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests absolute removal of continuous semantic penalties.                                                                                                                               </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-10-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-10-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>HSF          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-10-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>HIGH_SIM_FLOOR_ABLATION         </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-10-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests how much a stricter similarity floor (</span><!-- l. 634 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle class='text'><mtext>sim</mtext></mstyle></mrow></msub> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mn>0.95</mn></mrow></math></span><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>) constrains attack success.          </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-11-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-11-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>ADD          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-11-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>        <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>ADDITIVE_ABLATION                 </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-11-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                 <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests the additive version of the reward, in contrast to the multiplicative version (Section </span><a href='#reward'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>2.2</span><!-- tex4ht:ref: subsec:reward  --></a><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>).                                                                                                 </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-12-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-12-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>      <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>NC           </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-12-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>    <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>NO_CURRICULUM_ABLATION         </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-12-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                                 <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests if curriculum learning is useful for convergence.                                                                                                                                 </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-13-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-13-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>LWL          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-13-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>       <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>LOW_W_LEN_ABLATION              </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-13-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                               <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Tests if a weak length penalty leads to verbosity hacking.                                                                                                                               </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-14-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-14-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>BRT          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-14-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>             <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>BERT_ONLY                          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-14-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                                 <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Single-router run against the RouteLLM BERT router.                                                                                                                                 </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-15-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-15-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>CHY          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-15-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>           <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>CHAYAN_ONLY                       </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-15-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                                     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Single-router run against the Chayan router.                                                                                                                                          </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-6-16-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-16-1' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>     <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>CSL          </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-16-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>           <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>CAUSAL_ONLY                       </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-6-16-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                                                              <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Single-router run against the RouteLLM causal LLM router.                                                                                                                            </span></td>

</tr></table>                                                                                                                                                                   </div>

</tr><tr id='TBL-8-12-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' colspan='5' id='TBL-8-12-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'> <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>Decoding</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-13-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-13-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Temperature                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-13-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>1.0    </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-13-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>1.0    </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-13-4' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>1.0    </span></td><td class='td10' id='TBL-8-13-5' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>    <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>1.1     </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-8-14-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-14-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Top-</span><!-- l. 886 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mi>p</mi></math></span>               </td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-14-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'> <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>0.95   </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-14-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>0.95   </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-14-4' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'> <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>0.95   </span></td><td class='td10' id='TBL-8-14-5' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>   <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>0.90    </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-8-15-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' colspan='5' id='TBL-8-15-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'> <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>Scheduler</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-16-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-16-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Scheduler Type                                                                                                                                                                                                                              </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-16-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'> <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Step   </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-16-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Step   </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-16-4' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'> <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Step   </span></td><td class='td10' id='TBL-8-16-5' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'> <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Constant</span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-8-17-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-17-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Step Size                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-17-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>10    </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-17-3' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>  <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>10     </span></td><td class='td11' id='TBL-8-17-4' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>   <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>5     </span></td><td class='td10' id='TBL-8-17-5' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>    <span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>–      </span></td>
</tr><tr id='TBL-8-18-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' colspan='5' id='TBL-8-18-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'> <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmb8t-x-x-80'>Shared Across All Runs</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-19-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-19-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Curriculum                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     </span></td><td class='td11' colspan='4' id='TBL-8-19-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>            <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Easy-to-hard</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-20-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-20-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Similarity Model                                                                                                                                                                                                                            </span></td><td class='td11' colspan='4' id='TBL-8-20-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>            <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>GIST + NLI</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-21-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-21-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Similarity Weight </span><!-- l. 897 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><msub><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mrow><mstyle class='text'><mtext>sim</mtext></mstyle></mrow></msub></math></span>   </td><td class='td11' colspan='4' id='TBL-8-21-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>1.0</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-22-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-22-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Routing Objective                                                                                                                                                                                                                          </span></td><td class='td11' colspan='4' id='TBL-8-22-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>       <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>Score delta + flip bonus</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-23-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-23-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Flip Bonus                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     </span></td><td class='td11' colspan='4' id='TBL-8-23-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>1.0</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-24-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-24-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Training Epochs                                                                                                                                                                                                                             </span></td><td class='td11' colspan='4' id='TBL-8-24-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>10</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-25-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-25-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'> Gradient Accumulation                                                                                                                                                                                                                 </span></td><td class='td11' colspan='4' id='TBL-8-25-2' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'>                <div class='multicolumn' style='text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>4</span></div>
</td></tr><tr id='TBL-8-26-' style='vertical-align:baseline;'><td class='td01' id='TBL-8-26-1' style='text-align:left; white-space:nowrap;'><span class='ptmr8t-x-x-80'>                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        </span></td></tr></table>                           </div>
<figcaption class='caption'><span class='id'>Table 5:  </span><span class='content'>Checkpoint  configurations  for  the  four  single-router  runs  (<span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>BRT</span>,  <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>CHY</span>,  <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>CSL</span>,  <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>CAG</span>;
abbreviations as in Table <a href='#configuration-abbreviations-used-in-tablesref-tabablationconfigs-andref-tabsinglerouterconfigs-rows-marked-with-indicate-runs-that-are-considered-as-our-final-models'>3<!-- tex4ht:ref: tab:ablation_legend  --></a>). Each was tuned independently against its target router. The dash for
the <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>CAG </span>step size denotes a constant (non-stepped) schedule.
</span></figcaption><!-- tex4ht:label?: x1440003r5  -->
   </figure>
   <h5 class='subsubsectionHead' id='training-logs'><span class='titlemark'>A.4.3    </span> <a id='x1-41000A.4.3'></a>Training Logs</h5>
<!-- l. 913 --><p class='noindent'>We report the raw training trajectories logged with <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>Weights &amp; Biases </span>for the two final multi-router
models (<span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT</span>, <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>AGG</span>) and for the ablation runs, as a function of the optimization step. We track four
quantities: the validation ASR (Figure <a href='#validation-asr-against-the-training-routers-over-training-steps-the-periodic-peaks-track-the-cyclic-router-schedule-one-tooth-per-router-phase-the-aggressive-run-agg-peaks-close-to-well-above-the-conservative-ft-and-the-ablations-span-the-range-in-between-srl-simultaneous-training-is-the-flat-curve-as-it-optimizes-all-routers-jointly-rather-than-cyclically'>3<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:valaasrllogs  --></a>), the similarity reward (Figure <a href='#exponential-moving-average-of-the-similarity-reward-over-training-steps-the-conservative-ft-keeps-similarity-higher-than-the-aggressive-agg-and-both-dip-in-antiphase-with-the-asr-peaks-of-figureref-figvalasrlogs-when-the-policy-pushes-hardest-on-routing-similarity-is-sacrificed-among-the-ablations-zwsa-zero-similarity-weight-collapses-toward-confirming-that-without-a-semantic-term-the-paraphraser-abandons-meaning-preservation-entirely'>4<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:simrrewardllogs  --></a>), the KL divergence to the
reference paraphraser (Figure <a href='#kl-divergence-to-the-reference-paraphraser-over-training-steps-log-scale-with-its-looser-kl-penalty-agg-plateaus-roughly-an-order-of-magnitude-above-the-conservative-ft-quantifying-how-far-each-policy-is-allowed-to-drift-from-the-pretrained-paraphraser-larger-kl-coincides-with-higher-asr-but-lower-similarity-making-this-a-key-knob-behind-the-attacksimilarity-tradeoff'>5<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:klddivllogs  --></a>), and the mean group reward (Figure <a href='#mean-group-reward-over-training-steps-both-ft-and-agg-follow-the-sawtooth-rhythm-of-the-cyclic-router-schedule-with-agg-reaching-higher-peaks-than-ft-among-the-ablations-two-outliers-stand-out-zwsa-spikes-highest-by-dropping-the-similarity-penalty-entirely-at-the-cost-of-semantic-collapse-while-add-converges-stably-near-due-to-the-ungated-summation-of-reward-components-inflating-the-raw-signal'>6<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:meanrrewardllogs  --></a>). A recurring feature of the
multi-router curves is a periodic “sawtooth” shape: routers are visited under the cyclic schedule
(Section <a href='#training-methodologies'>2.3<!-- tex4ht:ref: subsec:training  --></a>), validation ASR rises while the currently targeted router is being optimized and drops
when the schedule rotates to the next one, so each tooth corresponds to one router phase of a
cycle.
</p>
   <figure class='figure' id='x1-41003r3'><span id='validation-asr-against-the-training-routers-over-training-steps-the-periodic-peaks-track-the-cyclic-router-schedule-one-tooth-per-router-phase-the-aggressive-run-agg-peaks-close-to-well-above-the-conservative-ft-and-the-ablations-span-the-range-in-between-srl-simultaneous-training-is-the-flat-curve-as-it-optimizes-all-routers-jointly-rather-than-cyclically'></span>
<div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 917 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 919 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/FINETUNED-AGGRESSIVE/ASR.png' /> <a id='x1-41001r1'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(a) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmr8t-'>Final models (</span><span class='ptmb8t-'>FT</span><span class='ptmr8t-'>, </span><span class='ptmb8t-'>AGG</span><span class='ptmr8t-'>).</span>                  </span></div></div>                                          <div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 924 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 926 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/ABLATIONS/ASR.png' /> <a id='x1-41002r2'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(b) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmr8t-'>Ablation runs (LSF, NFB, NNLI, SRL, ZWSA,
HSF, ADD, NC, LWL).</span>                      </span></div></div>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'>Figure 3: </span><span class='content'>Validation ASR against the training routers over training steps. The periodic peaks track
the cyclic router schedule (one tooth per router phase). The aggressive run (<span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>AGG</span>) peaks close to
<!-- l. 930 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mn>1.0</mn></mrow></math></span>,
well above the conservative <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT </span>(<!-- l. 930 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∼</mo><mspace class='negthinspace' width='-0.17em'></mspace><mn>0.5</mn></mrow></math></span>),
and the ablations span the range in between. <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>SRL </span>(simultaneous training) is the flat curve, as it
optimizes all routers jointly rather than cyclically.
</span></div>
   </figure>
   <figure class='figure' id='x1-41006r4'><span id='exponential-moving-average-of-the-similarity-reward-over-training-steps-the-conservative-ft-keeps-similarity-higher-than-the-aggressive-agg-and-both-dip-in-antiphase-with-the-asr-peaks-of-figureref-figvalasrlogs-when-the-policy-pushes-hardest-on-routing-similarity-is-sacrificed-among-the-ablations-zwsa-zero-similarity-weight-collapses-toward-confirming-that-without-a-semantic-term-the-paraphraser-abandons-meaning-preservation-entirely'></span>
<div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 936 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 938 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/FINETUNED-AGGRESSIVE/SIMR.png' /> <a id='x1-41004r1'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(a) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmr8t-'>Final models (</span><span class='ptmb8t-'>FT</span><span class='ptmr8t-'>, </span><span class='ptmb8t-'>AGG</span><span class='ptmr8t-'>).</span>                  </span></div></div>                                          <div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 943 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 945 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/ABLATIONS/SIMR.png' /> <a id='x1-41005r2'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(b) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmr8t-'>Ablation runs (LSF, NFB, NNLI, SRL, ZWSA,
HSF, ADD, NC, LWL).</span>                      </span></div></div>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'>Figure 4: </span><span class='content'>Exponential moving average (<!-- l. 949 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mi>α</mi> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mn>0.7</mn></mrow></math></span>)
of  the  similarity  reward  over  training  steps.  The  conservative  <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT  </span>keeps  similarity  higher
(<!-- l. 949 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∼</mo><mspace class='negthinspace' width='-0.17em'></mspace><mn>0.96</mn></mrow></math></span>)
than the aggressive <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>AGG </span>(<!-- l. 949 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∼</mo><mspace class='negthinspace' width='-0.17em'></mspace><mn>0.94</mn></mrow></math></span>),
and       both       dip       in       anti-phase       with       the       ASR       peaks       of       Figure <a href='#validation-asr-against-the-training-routers-over-training-steps-the-periodic-peaks-track-the-cyclic-router-schedule-one-tooth-per-router-phase-the-aggressive-run-agg-peaks-close-to-well-above-the-conservative-ft-and-the-ablations-span-the-range-in-between-srl-simultaneous-training-is-the-flat-curve-as-it-optimizes-all-routers-jointly-rather-than-cyclically'>3<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:valaasrllogs  --></a>:
when       the       policy       pushes       hardest       on       routing,       similarity       is       sacrificed.
Among      the      ablations,      <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>ZWSA      </span>(zero      similarity      weight)      collapses      toward
<!-- l. 949 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow> <mo class='MathClass-bin' stretchy='false'>−</mo> <mn>1</mn></mrow></math></span>,
confirming that without a semantic term the paraphraser abandons meaning preservation entirely.
</span></div>
   </figure>
<!-- l. 953 --><p class='indent'>
</p>
   <figure class='figure' id='x1-41009r5'><span id='kl-divergence-to-the-reference-paraphraser-over-training-steps-log-scale-with-its-looser-kl-penalty-agg-plateaus-roughly-an-order-of-magnitude-above-the-conservative-ft-quantifying-how-far-each-policy-is-allowed-to-drift-from-the-pretrained-paraphraser-larger-kl-coincides-with-higher-asr-but-lower-similarity-making-this-a-key-knob-behind-the-attacksimilarity-tradeoff'></span>
<div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 957 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 959 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/FINETUNED-AGGRESSIVE/KLD.png' /> <a id='x1-41007r1'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(a) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmr8t-'>Final models (</span><span class='ptmb8t-'>FT</span><span class='ptmr8t-'>, </span><span class='ptmb8t-'>AGG</span><span class='ptmr8t-'>).</span>                  </span></div></div>                                          <div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 964 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 966 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/ABLATIONS/KL.png' /> <a id='x1-41008r2'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(b) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmr8t-'>Ablation runs (LSF, NFB, NNLI, SRL, ZWSA,
HSF, ADD, NC, LWL).</span>                      </span></div></div>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'>Figure 5: </span><span class='content'>KL divergence to the reference paraphraser over training steps (log scale). With its looser
KL penalty (<!-- l. 970 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mi>β</mi> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mn>0.01</mn></mrow></math></span>),
<span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>AGG     </span>plateaus     roughly     an     order     of     magnitude     above     the     conservative     <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT</span>
(<!-- l. 970 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mi>β</mi> <mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>=</mo> <mn>0.05</mn></mrow></math></span>),
quantifying how far each policy is allowed to drift from the pretrained paraphraser. Larger KL
coincides with higher ASR but lower similarity, making this a key knob behind the attack/similarity
trade-off.
</span></div>
   </figure>
   <figure class='figure' id='x1-41012r6'><span id='mean-group-reward-over-training-steps-both-ft-and-agg-follow-the-sawtooth-rhythm-of-the-cyclic-router-schedule-with-agg-reaching-higher-peaks-than-ft-among-the-ablations-two-outliers-stand-out-zwsa-spikes-highest-by-dropping-the-similarity-penalty-entirely-at-the-cost-of-semantic-collapse-while-add-converges-stably-near-due-to-the-ungated-summation-of-reward-components-inflating-the-raw-signal'></span>
<div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 976 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 978 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/FINETUNED-AGGRESSIVE/MeanR.png' /> <a id='x1-41010r1'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(a) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmr8t-'>Final models (</span><span class='ptmb8t-'>FT</span><span class='ptmr8t-'>, </span><span class='ptmb8t-'>AGG</span><span class='ptmr8t-'>).</span>                  </span></div></div>                                          <div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 983 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 985 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/ABLATIONS/MeanR.png' /> <a id='x1-41011r2'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(b) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmr8t-'>Ablation runs (LSF, NFB, NNLI, SRL, ZWSA,
HSF, ADD, NC, LWL).</span>                      </span></div></div>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'>Figure 6:   </span><span class='content'>Mean   group   reward   over   training   steps.   Both   <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT   </span>and   <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>AGG   </span>follow   the
sawtooth    rhythm    of    the    cyclic    router    schedule,    with    <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>AGG    </span>reaching    higher    peaks
(<!-- l. 989 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∼</mo><mn>0.9</mn></mrow></math></span>)
than <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT </span>(<!-- l. 989 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mo class='MathClass-rel' stretchy='false'>∼</mo><mn>0.5</mn></mrow></math></span>).
Among  the  ablations,  two  outliers  stand  out:  <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>ZWSA  </span>spikes  highest  by  dropping  the  similarity
penalty   entirely   (at   the   cost   of   semantic   collapse),   while   <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>ADD   </span>converges   stably   near
<!-- l. 989 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mn>1.0</mn></mrow></math></span>
due to the ungated summation of reward components inflating the raw signal.
</span></div>
   </figure>
<!-- l. 993 --><p class='indent'>   The four trajectories jointly summarize the optimization dynamics: ASR (Figure <a href='#validation-asr-against-the-training-routers-over-training-steps-the-periodic-peaks-track-the-cyclic-router-schedule-one-tooth-per-router-phase-the-aggressive-run-agg-peaks-close-to-well-above-the-conservative-ft-and-the-ablations-span-the-range-in-between-srl-simultaneous-training-is-the-flat-curve-as-it-optimizes-all-routers-jointly-rather-than-cyclically'>3<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:valaasrllogs  --></a>), similarity
(Figure <a href='#exponential-moving-average-of-the-similarity-reward-over-training-steps-the-conservative-ft-keeps-similarity-higher-than-the-aggressive-agg-and-both-dip-in-antiphase-with-the-asr-peaks-of-figureref-figvalasrlogs-when-the-policy-pushes-hardest-on-routing-similarity-is-sacrificed-among-the-ablations-zwsa-zero-similarity-weight-collapses-toward-confirming-that-without-a-semantic-term-the-paraphraser-abandons-meaning-preservation-entirely'>4<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:simrrewardllogs  --></a>), KL divergence (Figure <a href='#kl-divergence-to-the-reference-paraphraser-over-training-steps-log-scale-with-its-looser-kl-penalty-agg-plateaus-roughly-an-order-of-magnitude-above-the-conservative-ft-quantifying-how-far-each-policy-is-allowed-to-drift-from-the-pretrained-paraphraser-larger-kl-coincides-with-higher-asr-but-lower-similarity-making-this-a-key-knob-behind-the-attacksimilarity-tradeoff'>5<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:klddivllogs  --></a>), and mean group reward (Figure <a href='#mean-group-reward-over-training-steps-both-ft-and-agg-follow-the-sawtooth-rhythm-of-the-cyclic-router-schedule-with-agg-reaching-higher-peaks-than-ft-among-the-ablations-two-outliers-stand-out-zwsa-spikes-highest-by-dropping-the-similarity-penalty-entirely-at-the-cost-of-semantic-collapse-while-add-converges-stably-near-due-to-the-ungated-summation-of-reward-components-inflating-the-raw-signal'>6<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:meanrrewardllogs  --></a>) evolve together, with the
aggressive configuration trading a higher KL and lower similarity for substantially stronger attacks and a
higher overall reward ceiling. The same trade-off, summarized at convergence rather than over time, is the
Pareto frontier of Figures <a href='#pareto-frontier-of-attack-success-rate-asr-vs-mean-semantic-similarity-on-the-routellmbert-indistribution-router-each-point-is-one-trained-checkpoint-the-dashed-line-connects-the-nondominated-front-marker-shapes-denote-run-category-baseline-reward-ablation-singlerouter-pretrained-the-xaxis-is-broken-to-isolate-the-zerosimilarityweight-outlier-zwsa-s-from-the-main-cluster'>11<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:pareto_bert  --></a>, <a href='#pareto-frontier-of-asr-vs-mean-semantic-similarity-on-the-chayan-indistribution-router-chayan-uses-a-different-decision-boundary-than-bertbased-routers-so-frontier-membership-differs-noticeably-across-the-two-indistribution-plots'>12<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:pareto_chayan  --></a>, <a href='#pareto-frontier-of-asr-vs-mean-semantic-similarity-on-the-routellmcausal-indistribution-router-the-aggressive-singlerouter-checkpoint-agg-anchors-the-highasr-end-of-the-frontier-reaching-the-highest-attack-rate-among-all-runs'>13<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:pareto_causal  --></a>.
</p>
   <h5 class='subsubsectionHead' id='reward-mean-trajectories'><span class='titlemark'>A.4.4    </span> <a id='x1-42000A.4.4'></a>Reward mean trajectories</h5>
<!-- l. 999 --><p class='noindent'>To complement the quantitative ablation tables, we report how the mean routing reward
(<span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>reward_route_mean</span>) evolves between the <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT </span>(FINETUNED) run and the <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>NC </span>(<span class='ptmri8t-x-x-109'>no curriculum</span>)
ablation. These curves are useful for comparing the smoothness of optimization between the two
trainings.
</p>
   <figure class='figure' id='x1-42003r7'><span id='routingreward-trajectories-rewardroutemean-for-the-baseline-finetuned-run-and-the-nocurriculum-ablation-over-training-steps-ft-a-shows-clean-periodic-spikes-aligned-with-the-cyclic-router-schedule-and-returns-to-a-stable-baseline-between-phases-whereas-nc-b-is-noisy-throughout-with-highamplitude-fluctuations-and-no-clear-structure-illustrating-how-curriculum-ordering-stabilizes-the-training-signal'></span>
<div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 1003 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 1005 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/MEAN_REWARD/FT.png' /> <a id='x1-42001r1'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(a) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmb8t-'>FT </span><span class='ptmr8t-'>baseline</span>                                              </span></div></div><br />
<div class='subfigure'>
<!-- l. 1010 --><p class='noindent'></p><!-- l. 1012 --><p class='noindent'><img alt='PIC' src='report/figures/MEAN_REWARD/NC.png' /> <a id='x1-42002r2'></a>
</p>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'><span class='ptmr8t-'>(b) </span></span><span class='content'><span class='ptmb8t-'>NC </span><span class='ptmr8t-'>ablation</span>                                             </span></div></div>
<div class='caption'><span class='id'>Figure 7: </span><span class='content'>Routing-reward trajectories (<span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>reward_route_mean</span>) for the baseline fine-tuned run and
the no-curriculum ablation, over training steps. <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT </span>(a) shows clean periodic spikes aligned with
the cyclic router schedule and returns to a stable baseline between phases, whereas <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>NC </span>(b) is noisy
throughout  with  high-amplitude  fluctuations  and  no  clear  structure,  illustrating  how  curriculum
ordering stabilizes the training signal.
</span></div>
   </figure>
<!-- l. 1020 --><p class='indent'>   In our interpretation, the FT curve serves as the reference optimization behavior, while NC isolates the
effect of removing curriculum learning from the training pipeline. The NC ablation exhibits substantially
noisier reward trajectories throughout training, with higher-amplitude fluctuations that persist well into
later steps, whereas the FT baseline settles into a much smoother and more stable signal, indicating that
curriculum learning is a key factor in stabilizing training.
</p>
   <h5 class='subsubsectionHead' id='results'><span class='titlemark'>A.4.5    </span> <a id='x1-43000A.4.5'></a>Results</h5>
<!-- l. 1027 --><p class='noindent'>Figure <a href='#testset-asr-of-every-run-rows-sorted-by-mean-asr-abbreviations-as-in-tableref-tabablationlegend-final-models-marked-with-against-each-router-columns-a-on-the-training-routers-chayan-is-broadly-vulnerable-and-routellmcausal-is-nearimmune-except-under-the-aggressive-agg-and-the-unconstrained-zwsa-b-on-the-heldout-routers-the-same-attacks-barely-transfer-only-r-is-dented-asr-while-mf-and-sw-stay-at-zero'>8<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:resultsaasr  --></a> shows that the <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>AGG </span>checkpoint is the strongest in-distribution model, reaching an ASR of
<!-- l. 1027 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mn>1.0000</mn></mrow></math></span> on <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>Chayan</span>,
<!-- l. 1027 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mn>0.7359</mn></mrow></math></span> on <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>RouteLLM-BERT</span>,
and <!-- l. 1027 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mn>0.1573</mn></mrow></math></span>
on <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>RouteLLM-Causal</span>. The conservative <span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>FT </span>checkpoint is consistently weaker,
especially on <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>RouteLLM-Causal </span>and <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>RouteLLM-BERT</span>, while the transferability heatmap
shows that the attack does not generalize well to unseen routers: ASR peaks at only
<!-- l. 1027 --><span class="report-inline-math"><math display='inline' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'><mrow><mn>0.0270</mn></mrow></math></span> (<span class='ptmb8t-x-x-109'>AGG</span>)
on <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>R2 </span>and is essentially zero on <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>MF </span>and <span class='t1-zi4r-0-x-x-109'>SW</span>. Overall, the aggressive setting improves attack strength on the
training routers, but largely at the expense of similarity between the original prompts and the paraphrased
ones as seen in Figures <a href='#pareto-frontier-of-attack-success-rate-asr-vs-mean-semantic-similarity-on-the-routellmbert-indistribution-router-each-point-is-one-trained-checkpoint-the-dashed-line-connects-the-nondominated-front-marker-shapes-denote-run-category-baseline-reward-ablation-singlerouter-pretrained-the-xaxis-is-broken-to-isolate-the-zerosimilarityweight-outlier-zwsa-s-from-the-main-cluster'>11<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:pareto_bert  --></a>, <a href='#pareto-frontier-of-asr-vs-mean-semantic-similarity-on-the-chayan-indistribution-router-chayan-uses-a-different-decision-boundary-than-bertbased-routers-so-frontier-membership-differs-noticeably-across-the-two-indistribution-plots'>12<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:pareto_chayan  --></a>, <a href='#pareto-frontier-of-asr-vs-mean-semantic-similarity-on-the-routellmcausal-indistribution-router-the-aggressive-singlerouter-checkpoint-agg-anchors-the-highasr-end-of-the-frontier-reaching-the-highest-attack-rate-among-all-runs'>13<!-- tex4ht:ref: fig:pareto_causal  --></a>.
</p>
   <figure class='figure' id='x1-43003r8'><span id='testset-asr-of-every-run-rows-sorted-by-mean-asr-abbreviations-as-in-tableref-tabablationlegend-final-models-marked-with-against-each-router-columns-a-on-the-training-routers-chayan-is-broadly-vulnerable-and-routellmcausal-is-nearimmune-except-under-the-aggressive-agg-and-the-unconstrained-zwsa-b-on-the-heldout-routers-the-same-attacks-barely-transfer-only-r-is-dented-asr-while-mf-and-sw-stay-at-zero'></span>
